Notwithstanding the current and potential benefits that these artifacts can bring to society at large, there are also concerns about potential misuses of them, either due to their technical limitations or ethical considerations.

"Complementary Material" means the accompanying source code and scripts used to define, run, load, benchmark or evaluate the Model, and used to prepare data for training or evaluation, if any. This includes any accompanying documentation, tutorials, examples, etc, if any.

Copyright (c) 2026 KDA Team, under the MIT License (LICENSE.kda-for-kda.txt).

The TIRx KDA kernels are adapted from NVlabs/kda (260927-kda-for-kda),
Copyright (c) 2026 KDA Team, under the MIT License (licenses/LICENSE.kda-for-kda).
The split kernel also retains its original Apache-2.0 TIRx-author attribution.
Source: https://github.com/NVlabs/kda/tree/260927-kda-for-kda

The experimental MoK training adapter (BF16 and MXFP8 routed experts) and CUDA
schedules are adapted from cursor/mixture-of-kittens, Copyright 2026 Cursor
Research, under Apache-2.0.
Source: https://github.com/cursor/mixture-of-kittens/tree/caeb2963f855c7ad53bb50c8bbf211086405cb98

The Qwen 3.5 and Qwen 3.8 Flash-Next gated attention blocks (flashinfer/experimental/qwen_3_5_block/_cudnn_frost/,
shared by flashinfer/experimental/qwen_3_8_block/) are a subset of the cuDNN Frontend Python package (python/cudnn
and one file of its test tree), Copyright (c) 2024-2026 NVIDIA CORPORATION & AFFILIATES, vendored byte-faithfully
with its import roots rewritten; each file keeps its own SPDX header: 67 files under the MIT License

"Apache-2.0 AND MIT". The pinned
revision, the per-file digests and the declared patches are listed in that directory's VENDOR.md and
VENDOR_MANIFEST.json.
Source: https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/tree/6b21be0e01ffe408c62399b4381d88639c06ddcd

The SM120 DSA indexer's single-CTA decode selection
(flashinfer/experimental/sm120_dsa_indexer/csrc/decode_select_row.cuh) is
adapted from SGLang's DeepSeek-V4 top-k (TopKRegister and
coarse_bin_lower_bound), Copyright 2023-2024 SGLang Team, under Apache-2.0.
Source: https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang (python/sglang/kernels/jit/include/sgl_kernel/deepseek_v4/topk_impl.cuh)

SGLang's original HC implementation, Copyright 2023-2024 SGLang Team,
under Apache-2.0. The low-M GEMM retains its original NVIDIA 2026 copyright
and FlashInfer PR #4266 provenance. The fixed-order batched GEMM retains

Doug Lea malloc (dlmalloc) is included in NVSHMEM in src/host/mem/dlmalloc.cpp.

The upstream source file states:

  This is a version (aka dlmalloc) of malloc/free/realloc written by
  Doug Lea and released to the public domain, as explained at
  http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/
